    "src/Components/commentSafeguard.ts": "export interface SafeguardMatch {\r\n  phrase: string;\r\n  index: number;\r\n  length: number;\r\n  category: 'slur_harassment' | 'creator_hostility' | 'profanity_insult' | 'spam_link' | 'shouting';\r\n  severity: 'block' | 'hold';\r\n  reason: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport interface SafeguardAnalysis {\r\n  isClean: boolean;\r\n  isBlocked: boolean; // True = hard-disable \"Post Comment\" (severe slurs/threats)\r\n  shouldHoldForReview: boolean; // True = highlight inline + shadowban to 'pending_review'\r\n  matches: SafeguardMatch[];\r\n  flaggedReasons: string[];\r\n}\r\n\r\ninterface RuleDefinition {\r\n  pattern: RegExp;\r\n  category: SafeguardMatch['category'];\r\n  severity: 'block' | 'hold';\r\n  reason: string;\r\n}\r\n\r\nconst SAFEGUARD_RULES: RuleDefinition[] = [\r\n  // 1. SEVERE SLURS, THREATS & SELF-HARM (HARD BLOCK — Cannot be submitted at all)\r\n  {\r\n    pattern: /\\b(kys|kill yourself|go die|neck yourself|hope you die|retard|retarded|faggot|fag|tranny|nigger|nigga|chink|spic|wetback|kike|whore|slut|cunt|rape)\\b/gi,\r\n    category: 'slur_harassment',\r\n    severity: 'block',\r\n    reason: 'Severe slurs, threats, and hate speech are strictly blocked. Remove highlighted words to post.'\r\n  },\r\n\r\n  // 2. DIRECT CREATOR ATTACKS & HARASSMENT (SHADOWBAN HOLD — Highlights inline & routes to 'pending_review')\r\n  {\r\n    pattern: /\\b(i hate you|hate you|you suck|this sucks|sucks ass|sucks so bad|fire the artist|worst artist|lazy artist|quit drawing|stop drawing|creator is lazy|horrible art|ugly art|waste of ink|worst manga|garbage series|trash series)\\b/gi,\r\n    category: 'creator_hostility',\r\n    severity: 'hold',\r\n    reason: 'Hostile phrasing toward creators or series detected. Posting as-is will hold this comment for moderator review.'\r\n  },\r\n\r\n  // 3. GENERAL PROFANITY, INSULTS & NAME-CALLING (SHADOWBAN HOLD — Highlights inline & routes to 'pending_review')\r\n  {\r\n    pattern: /\\b(stupid|stupid ass|dumbass|dumb|idiot|moron|loser|pathetic|worthless|useless|trash|garbage|rubbish|dogshit|horseshit|bullshit|shit|shitty|fuck|fucking|fucked|fucker|motherfucker|bitch|ass|asshole|dick|dickhead|piss off|stfu|wtf|crap|crappy|hella mid|mid af|boring af|unreadable|fell off|rip off|ripoff)\\b/gi,\r\n    category: 'profanity_insult',\r\n    severity: 'hold',\r\n    reason: 'Profanity or insulting language detected. Rephrase constructively, or your comment will be held for moderator review.'\r\n  },\r\n\r\n  // 4. EXTERNAL LINKS & SPAM (SHADOWBAN HOLD)\r\n  {\r\n    pattern: /(https?:\\/\\/[^\\s]+|www\\.[^\\s]+|discord\\.gg\\/[^\\s]+|t\\.me\\/[^\\s]+)/gi,\r\n    category: 'spam_link',\r\n    severity: 'hold',\r\n    reason: 'External links require moderator approval before appearing publicly.'\r\n  }\r\n];\r\n\r\nexport function analyzeCommentText(text: string): SafeguardAnalysis {\r\n  const raw = String(text || '');\r\n  if (!raw.trim()) {\r\n    return {\r\n      isClean: true,\r\n      isBlocked: false,\r\n      shouldHoldForReview: false,\r\n      matches: [],\r\n      flaggedReasons: []\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const rawMatches: SafeguardMatch[] = [];\r\n\r\n  for (const rule of SAFEGUARD_RULES) {\r\n    const regex = new RegExp(rule.pattern.source, rule.pattern.flags);\r\n    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;\r\n    while ((match = regex.exec(raw)) !== null) {\r\n      rawMatches.push({\r\n        phrase: match[0],\r\n        index: match.index,\r\n        length: match[0].length,\r\n        category: rule.category,\r\n        severity: rule.severity,\r\n        reason: rule.reason\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Sort by start index, then prefer longer matches if two rules start at the same index\r\n  rawMatches.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index || b.length - a.length);\r\n\r\n  // Deduplicate overlapping matches\r\n  const matches: SafeguardMatch[] = [];\r\n  let lastEnd = -1;\r\n  for (const m of rawMatches) {\r\n    if (m.index >= lastEnd) {\r\n      matches.push(m);\r\n      lastEnd = m.index + m.length;\r\n    } else if (m.severity === 'block' && matches.length > 0 && matches[matches.length - 1].severity !== 'block') {\r\n      matches[matches.length - 1] = m;\r\n      lastEnd = m.index + m.length;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Check for excessive ALL-CAPS shouting (> 25 letters and > 70% uppercase)\r\n  const lettersOnly = raw.replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, '');\r\n  if (lettersOnly.length >= 25) {\r\n    const upperCount = raw.replace(/[^A-Z]/g, '').length;\r\n    if (upperCount / lettersOnly.length > 0.7) {\r\n      matches.push({\r\n        phrase: 'ALL-CAPS SHOUTING',\r\n        index: 0,\r\n        length: raw.length,\r\n        category: 'shouting',\r\n        severity: 'hold',\r\n        reason: 'Excessive ALL-CAPS shouting detected.'\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const isBlocked = matches.some(m => m.severity === 'block');\r\n  const shouldHoldForReview = !isBlocked && matches.some(m => m.severity === 'hold');\r\n  const flaggedReasons = Array.from(\r\n    new Set(matches.map(m => `${m.category.toUpperCase()}: \"${m.phrase}\"`))\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    isClean: matches.length === 0,\r\n    isBlocked,\r\n    shouldHoldForReview,\r\n    matches,\r\n    flaggedReasons\r\n  };\r\n}\r\n\r\nexport function buildHighlightedSegments(\r\n  text: string,\r\n  matches: SafeguardMatch[]\r\n): Array<{ text: string; match?: SafeguardMatch }> {\r\n  const inlineMatches = matches.filter(m => m.length > 0 && m.phrase !== 'ALL-CAPS SHOUTING');\r\n  if (inlineMatches.length === 0) {\r\n    return [{ text }];\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  const segments: Array<{ text: string; match?: SafeguardMatch }> = [];\r\n  let cursor = 0;\r\n\r\n  for (const m of inlineMatches) {\r\n    if (m.index < cursor) continue;\r\n    if (m.index > cursor) {\r\n      segments.push({ text: text.slice(cursor, m.index) });\r\n    }\r\n    segments.push({\r\n      text: text.slice(m.index, m.index + m.length),\r\n      match: m\r\n    });\r\n    cursor = m.index + m.length;\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (cursor < text.length) {\r\n    segments.push({ text: text.slice(cursor) });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return segments;\r\n}",

    "src/Components/UploadTools.tsx": "import React, { useRef, useState } from 'react';\r\nimport Cropper from 'react-easy-crop';\r\nimport { X } from 'lucide-react';\r\nimport { supabase } from '../supabase';\r\n\r\nconst compressImage = async (file: any, maxDimension = 1200) => {\r\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\r\n    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { resolve(file); return; }\r\n    const reader = new FileReader();\r\n    reader.readAsDataURL(file);\r\n    reader.onload = (event: any) => {\r\n      const img = new Image();\r\n      img.src = event.target.result;\r\n      img.onload = () => {\r\n        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\r\n        let width = img.width; let height = img.height;\r\n        if (width > height && width > maxDimension) { height *= maxDimension / width; width = maxDimension; } \r\n        else if (height > maxDimension) { width *= maxDimension / height; height = maxDimension; }\r\n        canvas.width = width; canvas.height = height;\r\n        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\r\n        ctx?.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);\r\n        canvas.toBlob((blob: any) => { resolve(new File([blob], file.name.replace(/\\.[^/.]+$/, \".webp\"), { type: 'image/webp', lastModified: Date.now() })); }, 'image/webp', 0.85);\r\n      };\r\n    };\r\n  });\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst uploadToSupabase = async (file: any, folderPath: any) => {\r\n  const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop();\r\n  const fileName = `${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15)}.${fileExt}`;\r\n  const filePath = `${folderPath}/${fileName}`;\r\n  try {\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.storage.from('saturday-am-vault').upload(filePath, file, { upsert: true });\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage.from('saturday-am-vault').getPublicUrl(filePath);\r\n    return publicUrl;\r\n  } catch (error: any) { alert('Error uploading file: ' + error.message); return null; }\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const Dropzone = ({ label, subtext, height = \"p-6\", multiple = false, folderPath = \"misc\", maxDim = 1200, onUploadComplete }: any) => {\r\n  const fileInputRef = useRef<any>(null);\r\n  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);\r\n  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const processFiles = async (files: any) => {\r\n    setIsUploading(true);\r\n    const urls = [];\r\n    for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {\r\n      const optimizedFile = await compressImage(files[i], maxDim);\r\n      const url = await uploadToSupabase(optimizedFile, folderPath);\r\n      if (url) urls.push(url);\r\n    }\r\n    setIsUploading(false);\r\n    if (onUploadComplete && urls.length > 0) { multiple ? onUploadComplete(urls) : onUploadComplete(urls[0]); }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div \r\n      onClick={() => !isUploading && fileInputRef.current?.click()}\r\n      onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setIsDragging(true); }}\r\n      onDragLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}\r\n      onDrop={(e) => { \r\n        e.preventDefault(); \r\n        setIsDragging(false); \r\n        if (e.dataTransfer.files.length) {\r\n          const filesArray = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);\r\n          processFiles(filesArray); \r\n        } \r\n      }}\r\n      className={`w-full bg-black border border-dashed rounded-lg flex flex-col items-center justify-center transition-all ${height} ${isDragging ? 'border-[#fe9a00] bg-[#fe9a00]/10' : 'border-zinc-700 hover:border-[#fe9a00]'} ${isUploading ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : 'cursor-pointer'}`}\r\n    >\r\n      <input \r\n        type=\"file\" \r\n        className=\"hidden\" \r\n        ref={fileInputRef} \r\n        multiple={multiple} \r\n        onChange={(e: any) => { \r\n          if (e.target.files.length) {\r\n            const filesArray = Array.from(e.target.files);\r\n            processFiles(filesArray);\r\n            e.target.value = null; \r\n          }\r\n        }} \r\n      />\r\n      {isUploading ? (\r\n        <span className=\"font-bold text-[#fe9a00] text-xs animate-pulse\">OPTIMIZING & UPLOADING...</span>\r\n      ) : (\r\n        <>\r\n          <span className=\"font-bold text-zinc-400 text-xs transition-colors hover:text-[#fe9a00]\">{label}</span>\r\n          {subtext && <span className=\"text-zinc-600 text-[10px] mt-1 text-center group-hover:text-[#fe9a00]/70\">{subtext}</span>}\r\n        </>\r\n      )}\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n};\r\n\r\n// --- CROPPER ENGINE ---\r\nconst getCroppedImg = async (imageSrc: string, pixelCrop: any) => {\r\n  const image = new Image();\r\n  image.crossOrigin = \"anonymous\"; \r\n  image.src = imageSrc;\r\n  await new Promise((resolve) => { image.onload = resolve; });\r\n\r\n  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\r\n  canvas.width = pixelCrop.width;\r\n  canvas.height = pixelCrop.height;\r\n  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\r\n\r\n  ctx?.drawImage(\r\n    image,\r\n    pixelCrop.x,\r\n    pixelCrop.y,\r\n    pixelCrop.width,\r\n    pixelCrop.height,\r\n    0,\r\n    0,\r\n    pixelCrop.width,\r\n    pixelCrop.height\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\r\n    canvas.toBlob((blob) => {\r\n      if (blob) resolve(new File([blob], \"thumb.webp\", { type: \"image/webp\" }));\r\n    }, 'image/webp', 0.85);\r\n  });\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const ThumbnailCropperModal = ({ \r\n  imageUrl, \r\n  uploadFolder = 'misc', \r\n  aspect = 1,\r\n  title = \"Crop Thumbnail\",\r\n  onCropComplete, \r\n  onCropAreaComplete,\r\n  onCancel \r\n}: any) => {\r\n  const [crop, setCrop] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });\r\n  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);\r\n  const [croppedArea, setCroppedArea] = useState<any>(null);\r\n  const [croppedAreaPixels, setCroppedAreaPixels] = useState<any>(null);\r\n  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const handleSave = async () => {\r\n    // If the caller wants the mathematical crop area without re-uploading\r\n    if (onCropAreaComplete && croppedArea) {\r\n      onCropAreaComplete(croppedArea, croppedAreaPixels, zoom);\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (!onCropComplete) return;\r\n\r\n    setIsProcessing(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      const croppedFile: any = await getCroppedImg(imageUrl, croppedAreaPixels);\r\n      const publicUrl = await uploadToSupabase(croppedFile, uploadFolder);\r\n      if (publicUrl) onCropComplete(publicUrl);\r\n    } catch (e) {\r\n      alert(\"Cropping failed.      \r\n      <div className=\"relative w-full max-w-2xl h-[50vh] sm:h-[60vh] bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl\">\r\n         <Cropper\r\n      setCroppedArea(area);\r\n             setCroppedAreaPixels(areaPixels);\r\n           }}\r\n           onZoomChange={setZoom}\r\n         />\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div className=\"w-full    onChange={(e) => setZoom(Number(e.target.value))} \r\n             className=\"w-full accent-[#fe9a00] cursor-pointer\" \r\n           />\r\n           <span className=\"text-[10px] font-black window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches || \r\n      ('standalone' in navigator && Boolean((navigator as any).standalone)) ||\r\n      document.referrer.includes('android-app://');\r\n\r\n    if (isStandalone) {\r\n      setIsVisible(false);\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const dismissedAt = localStorage.getItem('am_install_dismissed');\r\n    if (dismissedAt && Date.now() - Number(dismissedAt) < 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const ua = setDeviceType('android');\r\n    } else {\r\n      setDeviceType('desktop');\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if ((window as any).deferredPrompt) {\r\n      localStorage.removeItem('am_pwa_installed');\r\n      setDeferredPrompt((window as any).deferredPrompt);\r\n      setIsVisible(true);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const handlePrompt = (e: Event) => {\r\n      e.preventDefault();\r\n      localStorage.removeItem('am_pwa_installed');\r\n      (window as any).deferredPrompt = e;\r\n      setDeferredPrompt(e);\r\n      setIsVisible(true);\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const handleCustomReady = () => {\r\n      if ((window as any).deferredPrompt) {\r\n        localStorage.removeItem('am_pwa_installed');\r\n        setDeferredPrompt((window as any).deferredPrompt);\r\n        setIsVisible(true);\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    const handleAppInstalled = () => {\r\n      localStorage.setItem('am_pwa_installed', 'true');\r\n      setIsVisible(false);\r\n      setDeferredPrompt(null);\r\n      (window as any).deferredPrompt = null;\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handlePrompt);\r\n    window.addEventListener('pwa-install-ready', handleCustomReady);\r\n    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);\r\n\r\n    return () => {\r\n      window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handlePrompt);\r\n      window.removeEventListener('pwa-install-ready', handleCustomReady);\r\n      window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);\r\n    };\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  const handleInstallClick = async () => {\r\n    const activePrompt = deferredPrompt || (window as any).deferredPrompt;\r\n    if (!activePrompt)     "src/Components/SplashIntro.tsx": "import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\r\n\r\nexport const SplashIntro = ({ onComplete }: { onComplete: () => void }) => {\r\n  const [isFading, setIsFading] = useState(false);\r\n  const isFinished = useRef(false);\r\n\r\n  const handleFinish = () => {\r\n    if (isFinished.current) return;\r\n    isFinished.current = true;\r\n    setIsFading(true);\r\n    \r\n    // Wait for the fade-out CSS animation to finish before unmounting\r\n    setTimeout(() => {\r\n      onComplete();\r\n    }, 800); \r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // THE FAILSAFE: If the video freezes or takes longer than 6 seconds, force it to skip.\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    const safetyTimer = setTimeout(() => {\r\n      console.warn(\"Splash video took too long. Auto-skipping.\");\r\n      handleFinish();\r\n    }, 6000); \r\n\r\n    return () => clearTimeout(safetyTimer);\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div \r\n      className={`fixed inset-0 z-[9999] bg-black flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-800 ease-in-out ${\r\n        isFading ?     "src/Components/useIntegrations.ts": "import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\r\nimport { supabase } from '../supabase';\r\n\r\nexport const useIntegrations = () => {\r\n  const initialized = useRef(false);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    // Prevent it from running twice in React Strict Mode\r\n    if (initialized.current) return;\r\n    initialized.current = true;\r\n\r\n    const initTracking = async () => {\r\n      try {\r\n        const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n          .from('app_settings')\r\n          .select('google_analytics_id, meta_pixel_id, tiktok_pixel_id')\r\n          .eq('id', 1)\r\n          .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n        if (error || !data) return;\r\n\r\n        // --- 1. => {\r\n        if (prev <= 1) {\r\n          setIsCompleted(true);\r\n          return 0;\r\n        }\r\n        return prev - 1;\r\n      });\r\n    }, 1000);\r\n\r\n    return () => videoRef.current.muted = true;\r\n          videoRef.current.play().catch(() => {\r\n            setVideoFailed(true);\r\n          });\r\n        }\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n  }, [isOpen, resolvedVideoUrl, videoFailed]);\r\n\r\n  const progressPercentage = useMemo(() => {\r\n    return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((totalDuration - secondsLeft) / Math.max(1, totalDuration)) * 100));\r\n  }, [secondsLeft, totalDuration]);\r\n\r\n  if (!isOpen) return null;\r\n\r\n  const remainingAfterThis = Math.max(0, maxDailyUnlocks - dailyUnlocksUsed - 1);\r\n  const hasActiveVideo = Clean up legacy beta keys from localStorage\r\n    try {\r\n      localStorage.removeItem(`am_tutorial_${tutorialId}`);\r\n    } catch (_) {}\r\n\r\n    // 2.     "src/Components/QuickReacts.tsx": "import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';\r\nimport { MessageSquare, X, Flag, User, Shield, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Lock } from 'lucide-react';\r\nimport { supabase } from '../supabase';\r\nimport { cleanText } from '../profanityFilter';\r\nimport { APP_ICONS } from '../appIcons';\r\n\r\n// --- THE SMART COMMENTS LIST (8 EMOJIS) ---\r\nconst SMART_COMMENTS = [\"🔥🔥🔥\", \"😱😱😱\", \"🤯🤯🤯\", \"🐐🐐🐐\", \"👀👀👀\", \"👏👏👏\", \"😭😭😭\", \"💯💯💯\"];\r\n\r\n// --- 1. THE LOGIC HOOK ---\r\nexport const useQuickReacts = (chapterId: string, currentPage: number, currentUser: any, onRequireAuth?: () => void) => {\r\n  const [localComments, setLocalComments] = useState<any[]>([]);\r\n  const [reportedReacts, setReportedReacts] = useState<number[]>([]);\r\n  const [showAllReacts, setShowAllReacts] = useState(false);\r\n  const [isReactInputOpen, setIsReactInputOpen] = useState(false);\r\n  const [reactText, setReactText] = useState('');\r\n  const [activeCommentIndex, setActiveCommentIndex] = useState(0);\r\n  \r\n  const [toastConfig, setToastConfig] = useState<{ message: string, type: 'error' | 'success' } | null>(null);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!chapterId) return;\r\n    const fetchReacts = async () => {\r\n      const { data } = await supabase.from('page_reacts').select('*').eq('chapter_id', chapterId).order('created_at', { ascending: true });\r\n      if (data) setLocalComments(data.map((r: any) => ({ id: r.id, pageIndex: r.page_index, user: r.user_name, avatar: r.avatar_url, text: r.text })));\r\n    };\r\n    fetchReacts();\r\n  }, [chapterId]);\r\n\r\n  const visibleComments = useMemo(() => localComments.filter(c => c.pageIndex === currentPage), [localComments, currentPage]);\r\n  \r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (visibleComments.length <= 1) return;\r\n    const interval = setInterval(() => { setActiveCommentIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % visibleComments.length); }, 3500);\r\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\r\n  }, [visibleComments.length, currentPage]);\r\n  \r\n  useEffect(() => { setActiveCommentIndex(visibleComments.length > 0 ? visibleComments.length - 1 : 0); }, [currentPage, localComments.length]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (toastConfig) {\r\n      const timer = setTimeout(() => setToastConfig(null), 3000);\r\n      return () => clearTimeout(timer);\r\n    }\r\n  }, [toastConfig]);\r\n\r\n  const submitReact = async (overrideText?: string) => {\r\n    if (!currentUser || !currentUser.id) {\r\n      if (onRequireAuth) onRequireAuth();\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const textToSubmit = overrideText || reactText.trim();\r\n    if (!textToSubmit || !chapterId) return;\r\n    \r\n    const safeText = cleanText(textToSubmit);\r\n    const newReactPayload = {\r\n      chapter_id: chapterId,\r\n      page_index: currentPage,\r\n      user_id: currentUser.id,\r\n      user_name: currentUser.name,\r\n      avatar_url: currentUser.avatar,\r\n      text: safeText\r\n    };\r\n    const tempId = Date.now();\r\n    \r\n    setLocalComments(prev => [...prev, { id: tempId, pageIndex: currentPage, user: currentUser.name, avatar: currentUser.avatar, text: safeText }]);\r\n    setReactText('');\r\n    setIsReactInputOpen(false);\r\n\r\n    try {\r\n      const { data, error: insertErr } = await supabase.from('page_reacts').insert([newReactPayload]).select().single();\r\n      if (insertErr) throw insertErr;\r\n      if (data) setLocalComments(prev => prev.map(c => c.id === tempId ? { ...c, id: data.id } : c));\r\n      \r\n      // Single authoritative update: +1 quick_reacts and +2 fandom_score\r\n      const { data: profile } = await supabase\r\n        .from('profiles')\r\n        .select('quick_reacts, fandom_score')\r\n        .eq('id', currentUser.id)\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n      if (profile) {\r\n        await supabase\r\n          .from('profiles')\r\n          .update({\r\n            quick_reacts: (profile.quick_reacts || 0) + 1,\r\n            fandom_score: (profile.fandom_score || 0) + 2\r\n          })\r\n          .eq('id', currentUser.id);\r\n        window.dispatchEvent(new Event('profileUpdated'));\r\n      }\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      console.error(\"Failed to save Smart Comment:\", err);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const reportReact = async (reactId: any) => {\r\n    if (!currentUser) { alert(\"Please log in to report content.\"); return; }\r\n    \r\n    const numId = Number(reactId);\r\n    if (reportedReacts.includes(numId)) return;\r\n    \r\n    setReportedReacts(prev => [...prev, numId]);\r\n\r\n    try {\r\n      const { error } = await supabase.rpc('submit_quick_react_report', { target_react_id: numId });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      setToastConfig({ message: 'Report submitted successfully.', type: 'success' });\r\n    } catch (err: any) {\r\n      setReportedReacts(prev => prev.filter(id => id !== numId));\r\n      setToastConfig({ message: `Failed: ${err.message || 'Error'}`, type: 'error' });\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  return { localComments, reportedReacts, showAllReacts, setShowAllReacts, isReactInputOpen, setIsReactInputOpen, reactText, setReactText, submitReact, reportReact, activeCommentIndex, visibleComments, toastConfig };\r\n};\r\n\r\n// --- 2. TOAST NOTIFICATION ---\r\nexport const QuickReactToast = ({ toastConfig }: any) => {\r\n  if (!toastConfig) return null;\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className=\"fixed bottom-24 sm:bottom-28 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-[6000] bg-black border border-zinc-700 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-full shadow-[0_10px_40px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] flex items-center gap-3 animate-fade-in pointer-events-none\">\r\n      {toastConfig.type === 'error' ? <AlertCircle className=\"w-4 h-4 text-red-500\" /> : <CheckCircle2 className=\"w-4 h-4 text-green-500\" />}\r\n      <span className=\"text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest\">{toastConfig.message}</span>\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n};\r\n\r\n// --- 3. TIMELINE BUBBLE & AVATARS ---\r\nexport const QuickReactTimeline = ({ mode, localComments, maxPage, currentPage, activeCommentIndex, onOpenDrawer }: any) => {\r\n  const timelineComments = useMemo(() => localComments.slice(-25), [localComments]);\r\n  const visibleComments = useMemo(() => localComments.filter((c: any) => c.pageIndex === currentPage), [localComments, currentPage]);\r\n  const activeComment = visibleComments[activeCommentIndex];\r\n\r\n  const percent = maxPage > 0 ? (currentPage / maxPage) * 100 : 0;\r\n  const shift = 50 - percent;\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <>\r\n      {timelineComments.map((comment: any) => (\r\n        <div \r\n          key={comment.id}\r\n          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onOpenDrawer(); }}\r\n          className=\"absolute pointer-events-auto cursor-pointer transition-transform hover:scale-125 drop-shadow-md z-[100]\"\r\n          style={mode === 'vertical' \r\n            ? { top: `${(comment.pageIndex / maxPage) * 100}%`, left: '50%', transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)', zIndex: comment.pageIndex === currentPage ? 100 : 5 } \r\n            : { left: `${(comment.pageIndex / maxPage) * 100}%`, top: '50%', transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)', zIndex: comment.pageIndex === currentPage ? 100 : 5 }\r\n          }\r\n        >\r\n          {comment.avatar && !comment.avatar.includes('pravatar') ? 'border-2 border-[#fe9a00] scale-125 opacity-100' : 'opacity-50 grayscale-[50%]'}`}>\r\n              <User className=\"w-2 h-2 sm:w-3 sm:h-3 text-zinc-400\" />\r\n            </div>\r\n          )}\r\n        </div>\r\n      ))}\r\n\r\n      {activeComment && (\r\n        <div \r\n          key={`active_${activeComment.id}`} \r\n          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onOpenDrawer(); }}\r\n          className={`absolute pointer-events-auto cursor-pointer z-[110] ${mode === 'vertical' ? 'left-full ml-3 sm:ml-4' : 'bottom-full mb-3 sm:mb-4'}`}\r\n          style={mode === 'vertical' ? { top: `${percent}%`, transform: 'translateY(-50%)' } : { left: `${percent}%`, transform: 'translateX(-50%)' }}\r\n        >\r\n          <div className={`animate-slide-${mode === 'vertical' ? 'right' : 'up'}-fade flex ${mode === 'vertical' ? 'items-center' : 'flex-col items-center'}`}>\r\n            {mode === 'vertical' && <div className=\"w-0 h-0 border-t-[5px] border-t-transparent border-b-[5px] border-b-transparent border-r-[5px] border-r-black/70 -mr-[1px]\" />}\r\n            \r\n            <div \r\n              className=\"bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-md text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-[180px] sm:max-w-[250px] border border-white/5 flex flex-col items-start hover:border-[#fe9a00]/50 transition-colors\"\r\n              style={mode === 'vertical' ? { transform: `translateY(${shift}%)` } : { transform: `translateX(${shift}%)` }}\r\n            >\r\n              <span className=\"text-[#fe9a00] font-semibold uppercase text-[8px] mb-0.5 w-full truncate\">\r\n                {activeComment.user.length > 15 ? `${activeComment.user.slice(0, 15)}...` : activeComment.user}\r\n              </span>\r\n              <span className=\"text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] leading-tight break-words text-left whitespace-normal\">\r\n                {activeComment.text}\r\n              </span>\r\n            </div>\r\n\r\n            {mode === 'horizontal' && <div className=\"w-0 h-0 border-l-[5px] border-l-transparent border-r-[5px] border-r-transparent border-t-[5px] border-t-black/70 -mt-[1px]\" />}\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n      )}\r\n    </>\r\n  );\r\n};\r\n\r\n// --- 4. Plus,\r\n  Trophy,\r\n  Users,\r\n  Newspaper,\r\n  Target,\r\n  PenTool,\r\n  Settings\r\n} from 'lucide-react';\r\nimport { useSeriesData } from '../userSeriesData';\r\nimport { supabase } from 'panel' : 'link'}`;\r\n    const storageKey = `am_share_rewards_${currentUser.id}_${todayKey}`;\r\n\r\n    let rewardedTargets: string[] = [];\r\n    try {\r\n      rewardedTargets = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(storageKey) || '[]');\r\n    } catch {\r\n      rewardedTargets = [];\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (rewardedTargets.includes(targetIdentifier) || rewardedTargets.length >= MAX_DAILY_REWARDED_SHARES) {\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    isAwardingRef.current = true;\r\n    try {\r\n      const { data: profile } = await supabase\r\n        .from('profiles')\r\n        .select('fandom_score, shares')\r\n        .eq('id', currentUser.id)\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n      if (profile) {\r\n        const updatedTargets = [...rewardedTargets, fandom_score: (profile.fandom_score || 0) + 5,\r\n            shares: (profile.shares || 0) + 1\r\n          })\r\n          .eq('id', currentUser.id);\r\n\r\n        setRewardMessage('+5 Fandom Self-Promotion'\r\n];\r\n\r\nconst SafeguardedCommentInput: React.FC<{\r\n  placeholder?: string;\r\n  submitLabel?: string;\r\n  autoFocus?: boolean;\r\n  isVisitor: boolean;\r\n  isMuted: placeholder = 'Share your thoughts on this series...',\r\n  submitLabel = 'Post Comment',\r\n  autoFocus = false,\r\n  isVisitor,\r\n  isMuted,\r\n  isCreatorOrStaff = false,\r\n  onRequireAuth,\r\n  onSubmit,\r\n  onCancel\r\n}) => {\r\n  const [text, setText] = useState('');\r\n  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);\r\n  const [showGuidelineBanner, setShowGuidelineBanner] = useState(true);\r\n\r\n  const analysis = useMemo(() => analyzeCommentText(text), [text]);\r\n  const highlightedSegments = useMemo(\r\n    () => buildHighlightedSegments(text, analysis.matches),\r\n    [text, analysis.matches]\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const blockingMatches = useMemo(\r\n    () => analysis.matches.filter(m => m.severity === 'block'),\r\n    [analysis.matches]\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const handleFormSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\r\n    e.preventDefault();\r\n    if (isVisitor) {\r\n      if (onRequireAuth) onRequireAuth();\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    if (isMuted) return;\r\n\r\n    const immediateCheck = analyzeCommentText(text);\r\n    if (!text.trim() || immediateCheck.isBlocked || isSubmitting) return;\r\n\r\n    setIsSubmitting(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      await onSubmit(text.trim(), immediateCheck);\r\n      setText('');\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setIsSubmitting(false);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div\r\n      className={`rounded-2xl PRE-POSTING COMMUNITY GUIDELINE BANNER */}\r\n      {showGuidelineBanner && (\r\n        <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-3 bg-[#fe9a00]/10 border border-[#fe9a00]/30 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5\">\r\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 text-left\">\r\n            <Sparkles className=\"w-4 h-4 text-[#fe9a00] shrink-0\" />\r\n            <p className=\"text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-zinc-200 leading-snug\">\r\n              <span className=\"text-[#fe9a00] font-black uppercase tracking-wider mr-1.5\">\r\n                Creator-First Community:\r\n              </span>\r\n              Support our creators! Please keep feedback constructive, respectful, and encouraging.\r\n            </p>\r\n          </div>\r\n          <button\r\n            type=\"button\"\r\n            onClick={() => setShowGuidelineBanner(false)}\r\n            className=\"text-zinc-400 hover:text-white p-0.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0\"\r\n            title=\"Dismiss reminder\"\r\n          >\r\n            <X className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\r\n          </button>\r\n        </div>\r\n      )}\r\n\r\n      {isMuted ? (\r\n        <div className=\"bg-red-950/40 border border-red-800/60 rounded-xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3 text-red-300 text-xs font-bold\">\r\n          <Lock className=\"w-4 h-4 text-red-400 shrink-0\" />\r\n          <span>\r\n            Your commenting privileges are currently restricted due to a community guideline violation.\r\n          </span>\r\n        </div>\r\n      ) : (\r\n        <form onSubmit={handleFormSubmit} className=\"space-y-3\">\r\n          <div className=\"relative\">\r\n            <textarea\r\n              value={text}\r\n              autoFocus={autoFocus}\r\n              onFocus={() => {\r\n                if (isVisitor && onRequireAuth) onRequireAuth();\r\n              }}\r\n              onChange={e => setText(e.target.value)}\r\n              placeholder={\r\n                isVisitor\r\n                  ? 'Sign in or create a free account to join the discussion...'\r\n                  : isCreatorOrStaff\r\n                  ? 'Post an official Creator / Staff comment or reply...'\r\n                  : placeholder\r\n              }\r\n              maxLength={1000}\r\n              rows={3}\r\n              className={`w-full bg-black border rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs sm:text-sm text-white placeholder-zinc-500 font-medium resize-none focus:outline-none transition-colors ${\r\n                analysis.isBlocked\r\n                  ? 'border-red-500 focus:border-red-400 bg-red-950/15'\r\n                  : analysis.shouldHoldForReview\r\n                  ? 'border-amber-500 focus:border-amber-400 bg-amber-950/10'\r\n                  : 'border-zinc-800 focus:border-[#fe9a00]'\r\n              }`}\r\n            />\r\n            <span className=\"absolute bottom-2.5 right-3 text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-600\">\r\n              {text.length}/1000\r\n            </span>\r\n          </div>\r\n\r\n          {/* 2. REAL-TIME INLINE SAFEGUARD HIGHLIGHTER & FRICTION ALERT */}\r\n          {analysis.matches.length > 0 && (\r\n            <div\r\n              className={`rounded-xl p-3.5 border transition-all animate-fade-in space-y-2 ${\r\n                analysis.isBlocked\r\n                  ? 'bg-red-950/35 border-red-500/70 text-red-200'\r\n                  : 'bg-amber-950/30 border-amber-500/60 text-amber-200'\r\n              }`}\r\n            >\r\n              <div className=\"flex items-start gap-2.5\">\r\n                {analysis.isBlocked ? (\r\n                  <ShieldAlert className=\"w-4 h-4 text-red-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5\" />\r\n                ) : (\r\n                  <Info className=\"w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5\" />\r\n                )}\r\n                <div className=\"space-y-1 text-left flex-1\">\r\n                  <p className=\"text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400\">\r\n                    {analysis.isBlocked\r\n                      ? 'Blocked Phrasing — Please Revise Before Posting'\r\n                      : 'Heads Up — Constructive Community Check'}\r\n                  </p>\r\n                  <p className=\"text-xs font-bold leading-relaxed text-zinc-200\">\r\n                    {blockingMatches[0]?.reason || analysis.matches[0]?.reason}\r\n                  </p>\r\n                </div>\r\n              </div>\r\n\r\n              <div className=\"bg-black/80 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-xs leading-relaxed font-medium text-zinc-300 break-words text-left\">\r\n                {highlightedSegments.map((seg, i) =>\r\n                  seg.match ? (\r\n                    <mark\r\n                      key={i}\r\n                      title={seg.match.reason}\r\n                      className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-black underline decoration-wavy ${\r\n                        seg.match.severity === 'block'\r\n                          ? 'bg-red-500/35 text-red-300 border border-red-500/60'\r\n                          : 'bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/50'\r\n                      }`}\r\n                    >\r\n                      {seg.text}\r\n                    </mark>\r\n                  ) : (\r\n                    <span key={i}>{seg.text}</span>\r\n                  )\r\n                )}\r\n              </div>\r\n            </div>\r\n          )}\r\n\r\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-1\">\r\n            <span className=\"text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest\">\r\n              {analysis.isBlocked\r\n                ? '🚫 Remove highlighted slurs/threats to enable posting'\r\n                : analysis.shouldHoldForReview\r\n                ? '⚠️ Flagged words detected — will be routed to moderator review'\r\n                : isCreatorOrStaff\r\n                ? '⚡ Posting with Official Creator / Staff Spotlight Flair'\r\n                : 'Be kind • Critiques are welcome, personal attacks are not'}\r\n            </span>\r\n\r\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\r\n              {onCancel && (\r\n                <button\r\n                  type=\"button\"\r\n                  onClick={onCancel}\r\n                  className=\"px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors cursor-pointer\"\r\n                >\r\n                  Cancel\r\n                </button>\r\n              )}\r\n\r\n              <button\r\n                type=\"submit\"\r\n                disabled={!text.trim() || analysis.isBlocked || isSubmitting}\r\n                className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-[11px] flex items-center gap-2 transition-all ${\r\n                  !text.trim() || analysis.isBlocked || isSubmitting\r\n                    ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-500 border border-zinc-700 cursor-not-allowed opacity-60'\r\n                    : 'bg-[#fe9a00] hover:bg-white text-black shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(254,154,0,0.3)] cursor-pointer'\r\n                }`}\r\n              >\r\n                <Send className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\r\n                <span>{isSubmitting ? 'Posting...' : submitLabel}</span>\r\n              </button>\r\n            </div>\r\n          </div>\r\n        </form>\r\n      )}\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const SeriesCommentsSection: React.FC<SeriesCommentsSectionProps> = ({\r\n  seriesSlug,\r\n  chapterId = null,\r\n  currentUser = null,\r\n  onRequireAuth\r\n}) => {\r\n  const [comments, setComments] = useState<CommentRow[]>([]);\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\r\n  const [replyingToId, setReplyingToId] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n  const [reportingCommentId, setReportingCommentId] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);\r\n  const [userProfile, setUserProfile] = useState<any>(null);\r\n\r\n  // Modal State for Creator / Staff \"Type BLOCK to Confirm\"\r\n  const [blockModalTarget, setBlockModalTarget] = useState<CommentRow | null>(null);\r\n  const [blockConfirmInput, setBlockConfirmInput] = useState('');\r\n  const [isExecutingBlock, setIsExecutingBlock] = useState(false);\r\n\r\n  const [likedCommentIds, setLikedCommentIds] = useState<string[]>(() => {\r\n    try {\r\n      return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('am_liked_comments_v1') || '[]');\r\n    } catch {\r\n      return [];\r\n    }\r\n  });\r\n  const [locallyReportedIds, setLocallyReportedIds] = useState<string[]>(() => {\r\n    try {\r\n      return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('am_reported_comments_v1') || '[]');\r\n    } catch {\r\n      return [];\r\n    }\r\n  });\r\n\r\n  const [resolvedAuthUserId, setResolvedAuthUserId] = useState<string | null>(\r\n    currentUser?.id || null\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    setResolvedAuthUserId(currentUser?.id || null);\r\n    supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data: { user } }) => {\r\n      setResolvedAuthUserId(user?.id || null);\r\n    });\r\n    const { data: sub } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {\r\n      setResolvedAuthUserId(session?.user?.id || null);\r\n    });\r\n    return () => {\r\n      sub.subscription.unsubscribe();\r\n    };\r\n  }, [currentUser?.id]);\r\n\r\n  // Hide bottom nav pill when Block Confirmation modal is open\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!blockModalTarget) return;\r\n    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('appOverlayActive', { detail: true }));\r\n    return () => {\r\n      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('appOverlayActive', { detail: false }));\r\n    };\r\n  }, [blockModalTarget]);\r\n\r\n  const effectiveUserId = currentUser?.id || resolvedAuthUserId;\r\n  const isVisitor = !effectiveUserId;\r\n\r\n  const isCurrentUserCreatorOrStaff = useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!userProfile) return false;\r\n    const role = String(userProfile.role || '').toLowerCase();\r\n    return (\r\n      ['admin', 'staff', 'moderator', 'creator', 'core_creator'].includes(role) ||\r\n      Boolean(String(userProfile.linked_creator_name || '').trim())\r\n    );\r\n  }, [userProfile]);\r\n\r\n  const isMuted = Boolean(\r\n    userProfile?.is_muted &&\r\n      (!userProfile?.muted_until || new Date(userProfile.muted_until) > new Date())\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const showToast = (msg: string) => {\r\n    setToastMessage(msg);\r\n    setTimeout(() => setToastMessage(null), 5000);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const fetchComments = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    if (!seriesSlug) return;\r\n    setLoading(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      let query = supabase\r\n        .from('comments')\r\n        .select('*')\r\n        .eq('series_slug', seriesSlug)\r\n        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })\r\n        .limit(200);\r\n\r\n      if (chapterId) {\r\n        query = query.eq('chapter_id', chapterId);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const { data: rawComments, error } = await query;\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n\r\n      const visibleRows = (rawComments || []).filter((c: any) => {\r\n        if (locallyReportedIds.includes(String(c.id))) return false;\r\n        if (c.status === 'rejected') return false;\r\n        if (c.status === 'pending_review') {\r\n          return Boolean(effectiveUserId && String(c.user_id) === String(effectiveUserId));\r\n        }\r\n        return c.status === 'approved';\r\n      });\r\n\r\n      const userIds = Array.from(new Set(visibleRows.map((c: any) => c.user_id).filter(Boolean)));\r\n      if (effectiveUserId && !userIds.includes(effectiveUserId)) {\r\n        userIds.push(effectiveUserId);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const profileMap = new Map<string, any>();\r\n\r\n      if (userIds.length > 0) {\r\n        const { data: profs } = await supabase\r\n          .from('profiles')\r\n          .select('id, username, avatar_url, is_premium, role, linked_creator_name, is_muted, muted_until')\r\n          .in('id', userIds);\r\n\r\n        (profs || []).forEach((p: any) => {\r\n          profileMap.set(String(p.id), p);\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (effectiveUserId && profileMap.has(effectiveUserId)) {\r\n        setUserProfile(profileMap.get(effectiveUserId));\r\n      } else if (effectiveUserId) {\r\n        const { data: myProf } = await supabase\r\n          .from('profiles')\r\n          .select('id, username, avatar_url, is_premium, role, linked_creator_name, is_muted, muted_until')\r\n          .eq('id', effectiveUserId)\r\n          .maybeSingle();\r\n        if (myProf) setUserProfile(myProf);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const enriched: CommentRow[] = visibleRows.map((c: any) => {\r\n        const p = profileMap.get(String(c.user_id));\r\n        return {\r\n          ...c,\r\n          author_username: p?.linked_creator_name || p?.username || 'Manga Fan',\r\n          author_avatar: p?.avatar_url || '',\r\n          author_is_plus: Boolean(p?.is_premium),\r\n          author_role: p?.role || 'reader',\r\n          author_linked_creator: p?.linked_creator_name || null\r\n        };\r\n      });\r\n\r\n      setComments(enriched);\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      console.error('Error loading comments:', err);\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setLoading(false);\r\n    }\r\n  }, [seriesSlug, chapterId, effectiveUserId, locallyReportedIds]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    fetchComments();\r\n  }, [fetchComments]);\r\n\r\n  const handleCreateComment = async (\r\n    content: string,\r\n    analysis: SafeguardAnalysis,\r\n    parentId: string | null = null\r\n  ) => {\r\n    if (!effectiveUserId) {\r\n      if (onRequireAuth) onRequireAuth();\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const initialStatus: 'approved' | 'pending_review' =\r\n      !isCurrentUserCreatorOrStaff &&\r\n      (analysis.shouldHoldForReview || analysis.flaggedReasons.length > 0)\r\n        ? 'pending_review'\r\n        : 'approved';\r\n\r\n    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;\r\n    const optimisticComment: CommentRow = {\r\n      id: tempId,\r\n      user_id: effectiveUserId,\r\n      series_slug: seriesSlug,\r\n      chapter_id: chapterId || null,\r\n      parent_id: parentId,\r\n      content,\r\n      status: initialStatus,\r\n      flagged_reasons: analysis.flaggedReasons,\r\n      likes_count: 0,\r\n      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n      author_username:\r\n        userProfile?.linked_creator_name || userProfile?.username || 'You',\r\n      author_avatar: userProfile?.avatar_url || '',\r\n      author_is_plus: Boolean(userProfile?.is_premium || currentUser?.tier === 'premium'),\r\n      author_role: userProfile?.role || 'reader',\r\n      author_linked_creator: userProfile?.linked_creator_name || null\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    setComments(prev => [optimisticComment, ...prev]);\r\n    if (parentId) setReplyingToId(null);\r\n\r\n    const { data: inserted, error } = await supabase\r\n      .from('comments')\r\n      .insert([\r\n        {\r\n          user_id: effectiveUserId,\r\n          series_slug: seriesSlug,\r\n          chapter_id: chapterId || null,\r\n          parent_id: parentId,\r\n          content,\r\n          status: initialStatus,\r\n          flagged_reasons: analysis.flaggedReasons\r\n        }\r\n      ])\r\n      .select('*')\r\n      .single();\r\n\r\n    if (error) {\r\n      setComments(prev => prev.filter(c => c.id !== tempId));\r\n      alert(error.message || 'Unable to post comment.');\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (inserted) {\r\n      setComments(prev =>\r\n        prev.map(c => (c.id === tempId ? { ...optimisticComment, ...inserted } : c))\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleDeleteComment = async (commentId: string) => {\r\n    if (!window.confirm('Delete this comment?')) return;\r\n    const prev = comments;\r\n    setComments(curr => curr.filter(c => c.id !== commentId && c.parent_id !== commentId));\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.from('comments').delete().eq('id', commentId);\r\n    if (error) {\r\n      setComments(prev);\r\n      alert('Failed to delete comment: ' + error.message);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  // Execute Creator & Staff Block + Delete after typing BLOCK in the modal\r\n  const executeConfirmedBlockAndDelete = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\r\n    e.preventDefault();\r\n    if (!blockModalTarget) return;\r\n    if (blockConfirmInput.trim().toUpperCase() !== 'BLOCK') return;\r\n\r\n    const comment = blockModalTarget;\r\n    if (effectiveUserId && String(comment.user_id) === String(effectiveUserId)) {\r\n      alert('Safety check: You cannot block your own Creator/Staff account! Use the Trash icon to delete your own test comment, or test the Block button on a reader comment.');\r\n      setBlockModalTarget(null);\r\n      setBlockConfirmInput('');\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    setIsExecutingBlock(true);\r\n    const prev = comments;\r\n    setComments(curr =>\r\n      curr.filter(\r\n        c =>\r\n          c.id !== comment.id &&\r\n          c.parent_id !== comment.id &&\r\n          c.user_id !== comment.user_id\r\n      )\r\n    );\r\n\r\n    try {\r\n      const { error } = await supabase.rpc('staff_creator_block_and_delete_comment', {\r\n        p_comment_id: comment.id\r\n      });\r\n\r\n      if (error) {\r\n        await supabase\r\n          .from('profiles')\r\n          .update({\r\n            is_muted: true,\r\n            muted_until: new Date(Date.now() + 3650 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString()\r\n          })\r\n          .eq('id', comment.user_id);\r\n\r\n        await supabase\r\n          .from('comments')\r\n          .update({\r\n            status: 'rejected',\r\n            flagged_reasons: [...(comment.flagged_reasons || []), 'BLOCKED BY CREATOR/STAFF']\r\n          })\r\n          .eq('id', comment.id);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      setBlockModalTarget(null);\r\n      setBlockConfirmInput('');\r\n      showToast(\r\n        `@${comment.author_username}'s comment was deleted and their account is now restricted from commenting app-wide until reviewed by moderation.`\r\n      );\r\n    } catch (err: any) {\r\n      setComments(prev);\r\n      alert('Failed to block user: ' + err.message);\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setIsExecutingBlock(false);\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleReportComment = async (comment: CommentRow, reason: string) => {\r\n    if (isVisitor) {\r\n      if (onRequireAuth) onRequireAuth();\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    setReportingCommentId(null);\r\n\r\n    const updatedLocalReports = Array.from(new Set([...locallyReportedIds, comment.id]));\r\n    setLocallyReportedIds(updatedLocalReports);\r\n    localStorage.setItem('am_reported_comments_v1', JSON.stringify(updatedLocalReports));\r\n\r\n    setComments(prev => prev.filter(c => c.id !== comment.id && c.parent_id !== comment.id));\r\n\r\n    try {\r\n      await supabase.rpc('report_community_comment', {\r\n        p_comment_id: comment.id,\r\n        p_reason: reason\r\n      });\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      console.warn('Report note:', err);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    showToast('Thanks for keeping Saturday AM constructive! Comment flagged for moderator review.');\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleToggleLike = async (comment: CommentRow) => {\r\n    if (isVisitor) {\r\n      if (onRequireAuth) onRequireAuth();\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    const alreadyLiked = likedCommentIds.includes(comment.id);\r\n    const delta = alreadyLiked ? -1 : 1;\r\n    const nextLikes = Math.max(0, (comment.likes_count || 0) + delta);\r\n    const updatedLikedIds = alreadyLiked\r\n      ? likedCommentIds.filter(id => id !== comment.id)\r\n      : [...likedCommentIds, comment.id];\r\n\r\n    setLikedCommentIds(updatedLikedIds);\r\n    localStorage.setItem('am_liked_comments_v1', JSON.stringify(updatedLikedIds));\r\n\r\n    const creatorDisplayName =\r\n      userProfile?.linked_creator_name || userProfile?.username || 'Creator';\r\n    const creatorAvatar = userProfile?.avatar_url || '';\r\n\r\n    setComments(prev =>\r\n      prev.map(c =>\r\n        c.id === comment.id\r\n          ? {\r\n              ...c,\r\n              likes_count: nextLikes,\r\n              creator_liked_by: isCurrentUserCreatorOrStaff\r\n                ? delta > 0\r\n                  ? creatorDisplayName\r\n                  : null\r\n                : c.creator_liked_by,\r\n              creator_liked_avatar: isCurrentUserCreatorOrStaff\r\n                ? delta > 0\r\n                  ? creatorAvatar\r\n                  : null\r\n                : c.creator_liked_avatar\r\n            }\r\n          : c\r\n      )\r\n    );\r\n\r\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc('toggle_comment_like', {\r\n      p_comment_id: comment.id,\r\n      p_delta: delta,\r\n      p_is_creator_or_staff: isCurrentUserCreatorOrStaff,\r\n      p_creator_name: isCurrentUserCreatorOrStaff ? creatorDisplayName : null,\r\n      p_creator_avatar: isCurrentUserCreatorOrStaff ? creatorAvatar : null\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    if (error) {\r\n      await [comments]\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  const repliesByParent = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const map: Record<string, CommentRow[]> = {};\r\n    comments\r\n      .filter(c => Boolean(c.parent_id))\r\n      .sort((a, );\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const renderCommentCard = (item: CommentRow, isReply = false) => {\r\n    const isOwn = Boolean(effectiveUserId && String(effectiveUserId) === String(item.user_id));\r\n    const const [isHidden, setIsHidden] = useState(false);\r\n  const [overlayCount, setOverlayCount] = useState(0);\r\n  const [resolvedUser, setResolvedUser] = useState<any>(currentUser);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (currentUser) {\r\n      setResolvedUser(currentUser);\r\n    } else {\r\n      supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => {\r\n        if (data?.user) setResolvedUser(data.user);\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n  }, [currentUser]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    const handleReaderToggle = (e: any) => setIsHidden(e.detail?.isOpen);\r\n    const handleOverlayToggle = (e: any) =>\r\n      setOverlayCount(prev => Math.max(0, prev + (e.detail ? 1 : -1)));\r\n\r\n    window.addEventListener('readerToggled', handleReaderToggle);\r\n    window.addEventListener('appOverlayActive', handleOverlayToggle);\r\n\r\n    return () => {\r\n      window.removeEventListener('readerToggled', handleReaderToggle);\r\n      window.removeEventListener('appOverlayActive', handleOverlayToggle);\r\n    };\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  if (isHidden || overlayCount > 0 || !resolvedUser) return null;\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className=\"fixed bottom-[calc(4.5rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] sm:bottom-[calc(6rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-full max-w-[340px] sm:max-w-[400px] z-[150] pointer-events-none flex justify-start gap-1.5\">\r\n      {/* ACTIVE WEEKLY HYPES (ORANGE) */}\r\n      <div\r\n        className=\"relative pointer-events-auto bg-zinc-950 border border-[#fe9a00] backdrop-blur-md px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full shadow-[0_5px_15px_rgba(0,0,0,0.9)] flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all hover:bg-black ml-2 sm:-ml-2 mb-1\"\r\n        title={`Active Weekly Hypes: ${hypesRemaining}/${rolloverCap} (Spent First)`}\r\n      >\r\n        <img\r\n          src={HYPE_FLAME_URL}\r\n          alt=\"Hype\"\r\n          className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5 object-contain animate-pulse drop-shadow-[0_0_5px_rgba(254,154,0,0.8)]\"\r\n          style={{ filter: 'url(#hype-flame-color)' }}\r\n        />\r\n        <span className=\"text-white font-black text-[11px] leading-none pt-0.5 tracking-wider\">\r\n          {hypesRemaining}\r\n        </span>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {/* VAULTED GOLD HYPES (METALLIC GOLD - UNCAPPED & PERMANENT) */}\r\n      {vaultedHypes > 0 && (\r\n        <div\r\n          className=\"relative pointer-events-auto bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-950/95 via-yellow-900/60 to-amber-950/95 border border-yellow-300 backdrop-blur-md px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(253,224,71,0.35)] flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1\"\r\n          title=\"Vaulted Gold Hypes (Uncapped & Never Expire!)\"\r\n        >\r\n          <div\r\n            className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5 bg-gradient-to-t from-amber-400 via-yellow-200 to-white drop-shadow-[0_0_5px_rgba(253,224,71,0.9)]\"\r\n            style={iconMaskStyle}\r\n          />\r\n          <span className=\"text-yellow-200 font-black text-[11px] leading-none pt-0.5 tracking-wider\">\r\n            +{vaultedHypes}\r\n          </span>\r\n        </div>\r\n      )}\r\n    </div>\r\n  );\r\n};"
  }
}
